### [1] [Can LLMs Really Understand Item Difficulty Levels? Implications for Automated Item Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28634)
*Xinyi Wang,Hong Jiao,Ming Li,Sydney Peters,Hanna Choi,Tianyi Zhou,Qingshu Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估大语言模型(LLM)在大规模阅读写作测试中预测题目难度的表现，对比了多种提示策略、参数设置，并与编码器模型(ConvBERT)及基于特征的监督学习模型比较。零样本 GPT-4.1 (temperature=0) 表现最好 (QWK=0.578)，但仍低于 ConvBERT (QWK=0.625)。所有 LLM 在识别难题上表现不佳，GPT-5.4 倾向于低估难度。嵌入降维显示不同难度题目嵌入混合，语义信息不足以预测难度。提示在使用 LLM 生成特定难度题目时需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 题目难度估计在形成性评价和高风险总结性评价中至关重要，但 LLM 在预测题目难度方面的能力尚未充分探索，需系统评估其表现并与现有方法对比。

Method: 使用大规模阅读写作测试题目，测试多个 LLM 在不同提示策略和参数设置下的零样本/少样本预测表现；对比编码器模型(ConvBERT)和基于特征的监督机器学习模型；采用二次加权 Kappa (QWK) 评估预测准确性；通过嵌入降维分析题目语义表示与难度的关系。

Result: 零样本 GPT-4.1 (temperature=0) 达到最高 QWK 0.578，但低于 ConvBERT 的 0.625；所有 LLM 难以识别高难度题目，GPT-5.4 倾向低估难度；嵌入降维显示不同难度题目嵌入混合，表明仅靠题目语义信息不足以预测难度。

Conclusion: LLM 无法准确理解基于实证数据的题目难度，且随着自身能力增强倾向将大多数题目视为简单，因此在利用 LLM 生成特定难度题目时应谨慎，需结合其他方法或人工校验。

Abstract: The estimation of item difficulty plays a key role in both formative assessment and large-scale high-stakes summative assessments. This study explores how large language models (LLMs) perform in predicting item difficulty levels using items from a large-scale Reading and Writing test. The study investigated various prompting strategies and parameter settings across multiple LLMs. LLM performance was compared with encoder-only language models and feature-based supervised machine learning models. Zero-shot GPT-4.1 with a temperature of 0 yielded the highest item difficulty level prediction accuracy, with a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.578. However, LLMs' prediction accuracy was lower than that of ConvBERT (QWK = 0.625), which outperformed the best feature-based supervised machine learning model. Further analysis showed that all LLMs struggled to label hard items; in particular, the current advanced GPT-5.4 tended to underestimate item difficulty levels. Dimension reduction of embeddings showed that item embeddings from different difficulty levels were mixed together, indicating that semantic information from items alone is likely insufficient for item difficulty level prediction. The findings suggest that if LLMs cannot understand item difficulty levels as evidenced by empirical data and tend to treat most items as easy when their own capabilities increase, caution should be exercised when using LLMs to generate items with targeted difficulty levels.

</details>


### [2] [Imbalanced Data Clustering via Targeted Data Augmentation Using GMM and LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28635)
*Noor Khalal,Abdallah Alaa-Eddine Djamai,Imed Keraghel,Mohamed Nadif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合高斯混合模型(GMM)和大语言模型(LLM)的无监督数据增强方法，用于解决NLP中欠代表性主题在无监督聚类任务中的捕获不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无监督NLP任务中，处理欠代表性主题具有挑战性，因为聚类算法往往无法充分捕获少数类主题，导致数据表示不平衡。

Method: 提出一种结合GMM和LLM的无监督数据增强方法：利用GMM的灵活性和鲁棒性检测对应欠代表性区域的聚类，然后使用LLM生成合成文档来丰富这些聚类，改善其表示。

Result: 在多个不平衡文本数据集上的实验表明，该方法在所有情况下都保持了聚类性能，且往往能增强聚类的可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法为无监督NLP任务中改善数据表示提供了一个鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In Natural Language Processing (NLP), dealing with underrepresented topics is challenging, especially in unsupervised tasks where clustering might not adequately capture minority topics. To tackle this challenge, our paper presents a novel unsupervised data augmentation method that integrates Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs). Due to their flexibility and robustness, GMMs can detect clusters corresponding to underrepresented areas in the data, while LLMs create synthetic documents to enrich these clusters and improve their representation. Experiments on various imbalanced text datasets demonstrate that our approach preserves clustering performance in all cases and often enhances cluster interpretability, offering a robust and scalable solution for improving data representation in unsupervised NLP tasks.

</details>


### [3] [Chain-of-Models: Cross-Model Auditing for Bias-Robust LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28636)
*Qian Wang,Zhanzhi Lou,Zhenheng Tang,Nuo Chen,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chain-of-Models (CoM) 自动化审计流程，让第二个模型在最终判断前检查第一个模型的推理轨迹，以缓解大语言模型作为自动评判时的认知偏差。实验发现：单模型的抗偏差能力不能预测其审计效果；最佳审计模型因偏差类型而异。基于功能多样性、单模型抗偏能力和校准后的审计效果，设计了按偏差类型选择审计模型的规则，在四个偏差切片上达到 0.884 准确率，优于固定单一审计模型 (0.824) 和无审计基线 (0.805)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用作自动评判，但其判断易受认知偏差影响。现有缓解方法多依赖提示词去偏，跨偏差类型鲁棒性差，或依赖人工评估，难以扩展。因此需要一种自动化、可扩展的审计机制来检测和纠正模型推理中的偏差。

Method: 构建 Chain-of-Models (CoM) 管道：第一个模型生成推理轨迹和初步判断，第二个模型（审计模型）检查该轨迹并输出最终判断。系统性探索审计模型身份（同一模型、同家族模型、不同家族模型）的影响。在 6 个模型家族的 9 个模型、4 种认知偏差（从众、权威、分心、阿谀）和 4 个事实数据集上进行实验。提出基于功能多样性、单模型抗偏能力和校准后审计效果的按偏差类型审计模型选择规则。

Result: 1. 单模型抗偏能力不预测审计效果：Kimi-K2.5 在多个偏差上单模型表现最强，却是 Qwen2.5-72B 有偏轨迹的弱审计模型。2. 最佳审计模型因偏差而异：GPT-4o 在从众、权威、分心偏差上最强，GLM-5 在阿谀偏差上最强。3. 提出的选择规则在校准/测试划分下，在四个有偏切片上达到 0.884 准确率，显著高于最强固定单一审计模型 (0.824) 和无审计基线 (0.805)。

Conclusion: Chain-of-Models 通过引入异构审计模型有效缓解了大语言模型评判中的认知偏差。审计模型的选择至关重要，且不存在通用的最佳审计模型；按偏差类型动态选择审计模型能显著提升整体判断准确性。该方法自动化、可扩展，为大语言模型作为评判者的可靠性提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly serve as automated judges, but their judgments remain vulnerable to cognitive biases. Existing mitigations mostly rely on prompt-driven debiasing, which is brittle across bias types, or human evaluation, which does not scale. We study \emph{Chain-of-Models} (CoM), an automated audit pipeline in which a second model inspects the first model's reasoning trace before producing the final judgment. The key design question is whether the auditor should be the same model, a same-family model, or a different-family model. Across 9 models from 6 families, 4 cognitive biases, and 4 factual datasets, we find that auditor identity matters in two ways. First, standalone bias resistance does not predict audit effectiveness: Kimi-K2.5 is the strongest standalone model on several biases, yet is a weak auditor for Qwen2.5-72B's biased traces. Second, the best auditor is bias-specific: GPT-4o is strongest on bandwagon, authority, and distraction, while GLM-5 is strongest on sycophancy. We operationalize these findings with a per-bias auditor selection rule that, given the bias type, scores candidates along functional diversity, per-bias standalone resistance, and calibrated audit effectiveness. Under a calibration/test split, the selector reaches the highest accuracy across the four biased slices ($0.884$ vs.\ $0.824$ for the strongest single fixed auditor and $0.805$ for the no-audit baseline). We release data, configurations, and an LLM-agent skill at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/chain-of-models-B585 .

</details>


### [4] [ZeroR@CHiPSAL 2026: Two-Stage Vision-Language Adaptation with Contrastive Learning for Nepali Meme Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28637)
*Nitiz Khanal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对尼泊尔语表情包多模态仇恨言论与情感检测的系统，基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型，采用两阶段训练流程（LoRA微调+对比学习微调），结合类别不平衡处理和集成推理，在CHiPSAL 2026共享任务中分别获得仇恨言论检测第2名（F1 0.797）和情感分析第4名（F1 0.518）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决尼泊尔语等低资源南亚语言表情包中的多模态仇恨言论与情感检测问题，避免传统OCR+翻译管道的误差传播，利用原生支持天城文的视觉语言模型实现端到端建模。

Method: 采用RA-HMD框架，基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct。两阶段训练：第一阶段使用LoRA微调配合MLP投影头进行生成式分类；第二阶段使用监督InfoNCE损失进行对比骨干网络微调。通过少数类过采样、图像增强和Focal Loss处理类别不平衡。推理时将第一阶段Token概率与第二阶段分类器得分按验证集调优权重集成。

Result: 在CHiPSAL 2026共享任务中，仇恨言论检测获得第2名（F1 0.797），情感分析获得第4名（F1 0.518）。提供了详细的消融实验、错误分析和适配大规模视觉语言模型到低资源南亚语言的见解。

Conclusion: 端到端方法利用模型原生天城文理解能力消除了OCR和翻译管道的误差传播，两阶段训练结合集成推理有效提升性能，为低资源语言多模态仇恨言论检测提供了可行范式。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for the CHiPSAL 2026 shared task on multimodal hate speech and sentiment detection in Nepali memes. We address both subtasks: binary hate speech classification and three-class sentiment analysis. Our approach adapts the Robust Adaptation of Hateful Meme Detection (RA-HMD) framework using Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, a state-of-the-art vision-language model with native Devanagari support. We employ a two-stage training pipeline: (1) LoRA fine-tuning with an MLP projection head for generative classification, and (2) contrastive backbone fine-tuning with supervised InfoNCE loss. We handle class imbalance through minority oversampling, image augmentation, and focal loss. At inference, we ensemble Stage 1 token probabilities with Stage 2 classifier scores using validation-tuned weights. Our end-to-end approach eliminates error propagation from separate OCR and translation pipelines by leveraging the model's native Devanagari understanding. Our system achieved \textbf{2nd place} on hate speech detection (F1: 0.797) and \textbf{4th place} on sentiment analysis (F1: 0.518). We provide detailed ablations, error analysis, and insights into adapting large vision-language models for low-resource South Asian languages.

</details>


### [5] [Learning Stateful Predictive Knowledge From Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28638)
*Yan Song,Xidong Feng,Bo Liu,Xinyu Cui,Haotian Fu,Zichen Liu,Mengyue Yang,Cheng Deng,Jian Zhao,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Stateful Knowledge Learning (SKL)，让LLM代理从轨迹级反思转向维护状态锚定的预测性知识，通过自蒸馏(SKL-SD)和强化学习(SKL-RL)两种算法实现，在WebShop、ScienceWorld和ChessPuzzles上显著优于现有反思范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理主要依赖轨迹级反思提取见解，这属于情景事后诸葛亮，而非预测性远见，导致生成的启发式规则脆弱且路径依赖，泛化能力差。

Method: 提出Stateful Knowledge Learning (SKL)，将代理重点从轨迹总结转移到维护Stateful Knowledge（显式、声明性、锚定状态的预测性评估）。引入两种算法：SKL-SD（自蒸馏）和SKL-RL（强化学习），训练代理自主从经验中提取状态锚定的预测性知识并用于决策。

Result: 在交互式环境WebShop、ScienceWorld及复杂推理任务ChessPuzzles上，配备SKL能力的模型显著超越基于反思的训练范式。

Conclusion: SKL通过显式的状态锚定预测性知识，解决了轨迹级反思的局限性，实现了更细粒度、可泛化且可自举的知识学习，为LLM代理的经验学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents increasingly learn from experience, they primarily rely on trajectory-level reflection to extract insights. Viewed through the lens of predictive knowledge, we argue that this approach operates on episodic hindsight rather than predictive foresight, yielding brittle, path-dependent heuristics. To address this, we propose Stateful Knowledge Learning (SKL). SKL shifts the agent's focus from trajectory-level summarization to maintaining Stateful Knowledge: explicit, declarative predictive assessments anchored to state. We first demonstrate a motivating example showing how stateful knowledge provides granularity, enhances generalization, and enables knowledge bootstrapping. To further scale up the idea, we introduce two algorithms via self-distillation (SKL-SD) and reinforcement learning (SKL-RL), training agents to autonomously extract state-grounded predictive knowledge from experience and learn to leverage it for policy making. Experiments on interactive environments (WebShop, ScienceWorld) and a complex reasoning task (ChessPuzzles) demonstrate that equipping models with the inherent ability to learn stateful predictive knowledge significantly outpaces current reflection-based training paradigms.

</details>


### [6] [The Asymmetric Effects of Knowledge Distillation on Bias in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28639)
*Plawan Kumar Rath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现知识蒸馏在小型指令微调语言模型中对偏见的影响具有不对称性：在明确任务（BBQ-disambig）上提升跟随上下文能力，将最偏基线的上下文覆盖错误率从44%降至24%；但在模糊任务（BBQ-ambig）上破坏逐项拒绝校准，导致15%原本正确拒绝的条目变为刻板印象回答。两效应幅度不相关，提示源自不同机制。聚合指标掩盖逐项危害，拒绝注入SFT易导致过度拒绝。论文提出逐条件校准诊断（PCCD）三步协议，能同时捕捉不对称危害与琐碎拒绝失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示知识蒸馏对小模型偏见的不对称影响，指出现有聚合评估指标无法捕捉逐项校准损害与琐碎拒绝失效，从而动机开发更细粒度的诊断协议。

Method: 采用Gemma-2-9B作为教师模型，对SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct与OLMo-2-1B-Instruct进行响应式蒸馏，在BBQ-disambig与BBQ-ambig任务上测量上下文跟随与拒绝校准；审计四个训练语料库中的拒绝回答比例；实验拒绝注入的监督微调（SFT）；提出并验证逐条件校准诊断（PCCD）三步协议（评估拒绝校准、上下文跟随、能力保持）。

Result: 蒸馏在明确任务显著降低错误率（44%→24%）；在模糊任务导致15%正确拒绝条目转为刻板印象回答，silence-loss为8%，filled-silence占新增偏见89%；两效应跨28配置不相关（Spearman ρ=0.19，不显著）。语料审计显拒绝回答形态<0.5%。拒绝注入SFT要么破坏解析，要么陷入琐碎拒绝（拒绝率99.8%，明确任务准确率0.2%），却被聚合指标判为完美校准。PCCD成功识别出不对称危害与琐碎拒绝失效。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏对偏见的影响由不同机制驱动的不对称效应组成，聚合指标掩盖逐项危害。拒绝注入SFT易过度修正。PCCD提供更全面的评估框架，能同时捕捉拒绝校准损失、上下文跟随提升与能力退化。

Abstract: We show that knowledge distillation in small instruction-tuned language models has asymmetric effects on bias. On unambiguous tasks (BBQ-disambig), response-based distillation from a Gemma-2-9B teacher improves context-following: for the most biased baseline (SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct), it cuts the context-overriding error rate from 44% to 24%. On ambiguous tasks (BBQ-ambig), the same distillation destroys per-item refusal calibration: 15% of items where the baseline correctly abstained instead receive stereotype answers, even when overall refusal rate is preserved. The pattern reproduces on a second student family (OLMo-2-1B-Instruct), with silence-loss of 8% and filled-silence accounting for 89% of new bias. Across the full 28-configuration grid, the magnitudes of silence-loss and filled-silence are uncorrelated (Spearman $ρ=0.19$, n.s.), indicating that the two effects arise from distinct mechanisms.
  Aggregate stereotype metrics (CrowS-Pairs, overall BBQ Stereotype Reliance Score) average over both effects and conceal the per-item harm. We trace the calibration loss to a data-side mechanism: an audit of four training corpora finds <0.5% refusal-as-answer-shape. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with refusal injection either breaks parsing or over-corrects into a trivial-refuser regime (refusal rate 99.8%, disambig accuracy 0.2%) that aggregate metrics would call perfectly calibrated. We propose Per-Condition Calibration Diagnosis (PCCD), a three-step protocol that evaluates refusal calibration, context-following, and capability preservation. PCCD catches both the asymmetric harm and the trivial-refuser failure mode that aggregate evaluations miss.

</details>


### [7] [TokenSwap: Benchmarking and Reducing the Modality Gap in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28640)
*Andong Hua,Colton Bishop,Igor Mordatch,Arian Hosseini,Jindong Gu,Aleksandra Faust,Rebecca Roelofs,Yao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现多模态大语言模型在语义等价的文本与图文交错输入上存在系统性性能差距（模态差距），提出 TokenSwap 方法构建图文交错基准 TokenSwap-Bench，评估 42 个模型发现平均差距 19.6%，推理模型差距较小，训练中引入 TokenSwap 可有效缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型应对语义等价的跨模态输入生成一致响应，但观测到系统性预测差异，即模态差距，需量化并缩小该差距以提升模型鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 TokenSwap，将文本概念替换为语义对齐的图像，生成视觉与文本 token 交错序列，将 MMLU 等文本基准转为图文交错版 TokenSwap-Bench，评估 42 个 MLLMs 在文本-only 与图文交错输入上的性能差异，并探索提示策略、扩大训练算力及训练中加入 TokenSwap 的效果。

Result: 所有模型均存在模态差距，性能下降 4.2%-47.4%，平均 19.6%±3.3%；推理模型平均差距 10.1%，非推理模型 25.5%；提示策略和单纯扩大训练算力不可靠；训练中融入 TokenSwap 能有效缩小差距，同时保持文本-only 和视觉-语言性能。

Conclusion: 模态差距是 MLLMs 普遍存在的问题，推理能力有助于缩小差距，但仅靠推理时干预或扩大规模不足以解决；在训练阶段引入 TokenSwap 是一种有效的缓解策略，可提升跨模态一致性而不损害原有能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) should generate consistent responses given semantically equivalent inputs across modalities. However, we observe a systematic discrepancy in model predictions under such cross-modal variations. Specifically, we define the modality gap as the difference in model performance under semantically equivalent textual and multimodal inputs. We introduce TokenSwap, a method that constructs such inputs by replacing textual concepts with semantically aligned images, resulting in sequences where visual tokens are interleaved with text tokens. Based on TokenSwap, we transform existing text-based benchmarks such as MMLU into image-interleaved counterparts, resulting in TokenSwap-Bench. Across 42 MLLMs, we observe a pervasive modality gap, with performance decreasing by 4.2% to 47.4% when moving from text-only to image-interleaved inputs, averaging 19.6% +/- 3.3% across models. Notably, we observe that reasoning models exhibit consistently smaller gaps, achieving an average gap of 10.1% compared to 25.5% for non-reasoning models. In contrast, neither prompting strategies nor scaling training compute alone reliably reduces the modality gap. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating TokenSwap during training effectively mitigates this gap while preserving strong text-only and vision-language performance.

</details>


### [8] [The Formalism Trap: Are LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluators Blinded by Consensus Mimicry under Social Load?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28641)
*Dahlia Shehata,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了'智能体形式主义陷阱'和评估不协和指数(D_E)，量化了LLM-as-a-Judge系统在对抗性负载下如何混淆结构性程序主义与语义真理。通过分析3个领域(GAIA、SWE-bench、Multi-Challenge)的22,500条轨迹，提取了幻觉操作的语义分类学，并通过确定性词法接地验证(p < 10^-120)。逻辑回归元评估器隔离了评估器捕获的确切句法触发因素(ROC-AUC 0.8779)，零样本留一域外迁移证明该漏洞普遍与领域无关(平均ROC-AUC 0.7482)。架构分析显示不同模拟蜂群拓扑诱导数学上不同的语义盲区，证明无锚闭环评估不稳定、系统性发散，需要架构特定的警戒过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示LLM-as-a-Judge评估系统的根本缺陷：当前评估方法倾向于将结构化的程序合规性误判为语义正确性，导致在对抗性条件下产生系统性的评估失效。作者旨在量化这种'形式主义陷阱'，并证明其跨领域的普遍性和架构相关性。

Method: 1. 构建评估不协和指数(D_E)量化形式主义与语义真理的偏离度；2. 在GAIA、SWE-bench、Multi-Challenge三个基准上收集22,500条评估轨迹；3. 提取幻觉操作的语义分类学，通过确定性词法接地验证统计显著性；4. 训练逻辑回归元评估器识别评估器捕获的句法触发因素；5. 实施零样本留一域外迁移实验验证跨领域泛化性；6. 分析不同模拟蜂群拓扑对语义盲区的影响。

Result: 1. 幻觉分类学通过确定性词法接地验证，显著性p < 10^-120；2. 逻辑回归元评估器识别句法触发因素达到ROC-AUC 0.8779；3. 零样本留一域外迁移平均ROC-AUC 0.7482，证明漏洞跨领域普遍存在；4. 不同蜂群拓扑诱导数学上不同的语义盲区，揭示架构特定的评估失效模式。

Conclusion: 无锚闭环评估本质上不稳定且系统性发散，LLM-as-a-Judge系统会将结构化程序合规性误判为语义真理。该漏洞具有跨领域普遍性，且不同架构拓扑产生不同的语义盲区。因此，必须实施架构特定的警戒过滤器，而非依赖通用的评估机制。

Abstract: We introduce the \textit{Agentic Formalism Trap} and the Evaluative Dissonance Index ($D_E$), quantifying how LLM-as-a-Judge systems conflate structural proceduralism with semantic truth under adversarial load. Analyzing 22,500 trajectories across 3 domains (GAIA, SWE-bench, Multi-Challenge), we extract a semantic taxonomy of hallucination maneuvers, validated via deterministic lexical grounding ($p < 10^{-120}$). A logistic meta-evaluator isolates the exact syntactic triggers of this evaluator capture (ROC-AUC 0.8779), while a zero-shot Leave-One-Domain-Out transfer proves the vulnerability is universally domain-agnostic (mean ROC-AUC 0.7482). Architectural profiling reveals that distinct simulated swarm topologies induce mathematically disparate semantic blind spots, proving that unanchored closed-loop evaluation is unstable, systemically divergent and necessitates architecture-specific vigilance filters.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating Federated Pre-Training: On the Reliability of Downstream Fine-Tuning and Intrinsic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28658)
*Claudia Grosser,Maike Heuer,Denis Krompass,Thomas A. Runkler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 联邦预训练评估中，下游微调不能可靠保留预训练排名，而直接的下一词预测与预训练测试困惑度强对应，建议使用更接近预训练目标的评估信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦预训练评估面临挑战：客户端参与差异和本地数据可用性使得直接可比评估困难；预训练测试困惑度与预训练分布绑定，而下游基准引入任务特定适应可能不忠实反映预训练建立的测试困惑度。

Method: 使用受控的中心化和联邦训练模型（16M参数Transformer，相同客户端数据），通过是否保留在相同预训练测试集上建立的参考排名来评估评估协议。比较GLUE上的下游微调（全量、仅头、减少数据变体）与GLUE文本上的下一词预测作为内在评估信号。

Result: 下游微调不能可靠保留预训练排名，而直接下一词预测与预训练测试困惑度表现出强对应关系。

Conclusion: 仅依赖下游微调比较联邦预训练模型可能具有误导性，更接近原始预训练目标的评估信号值得更多关注。

Abstract: Federated pre-training offers a way to train foundation models on private or distributed data without centralizing the underlying datasets. However, evaluating federated pre-training remains challenging because differences in client participation and local data availability can make directly comparable evaluation difficult. Moreover, pre-training test perplexity is tied to the pre-training distribution, while downstream benchmarks introduce task-specific adaptation that may not faithfully reflect the test perplexity established during pre-training. In this work, we study which evaluation protocol more reliably reflects federated pre-training quality. Using a controlled set of centralized and federated-trained models of a 16M parameter transformer model trained on identical client data, we assess evaluation protocols by whether they preserve a reference ranking established on the same pre-training testset. We compare downstream fine-tuning on GLUE, including full, head-only, and reduced-data variants, with next-token prediction on GLUE text as an intrinsic evaluation signal. Our results show that downstream fine-tuning does not reliably preserve the pre-training ranking, whereas direct next-token prediction exhibits a strong correspondence with the pre-training test perplexity. These findings suggest that downstream fine-tuning alone can be misleading when comparing federated pre-trained models, and that evaluation signals closer to the original pre-training objective deserve greater attention.

</details>


### [10] [Are the Financial Reasoning from LLMs Credible? A Real World Test over Long-Horizon Statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28661)
*Xinke Tong,Xuanming Zhang,Tianyi Tang,An Yang,Jiatu Hu,Guojie Lin,Zhenzhen Shi,Lingfeng Zeng,Boyu Yang,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出FinIndices基准测试大语言模型在财务报表上的结构化推理能力，发现模型存在“知识瓶颈”和“结构瓶颈”，监督微调可部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法捕捉真实工业复杂性，多依赖选择题或单跳问答，忽略跨报表动态和时间去累积，财务领域需数值精度和多步逻辑，是理想测试床。

Method: 构建FinIndices基准，包含单指标计算和表格指标制表任务，利用自动化合成管道和对抗性陷阱，评估模型在未裁剪财务报表（高达32K tokens）上的数据处理保真度。

Result: 评估揭示两大漏洞：知识瓶颈（移除公式提示导致性能崩塌，如Gemini-3.1-Pro在表格任务从70.70%降至38.22%），结构瓶颈（生成多指标多周期表格的认知负荷耗尽推理能力，导致浅层启发式错误）；SFT带来零提示下显著提升（单指标+8.54%，表格+3.82%）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在财务结构化推理上存在根本缺陷，依赖表面模式匹配而非真实推理；结构化逻辑可通过数据中心对齐部分恢复，但仍需解决时间去累积和存量流量口径不匹配等核心问题。

Abstract: Do Large Language Models (LLMs) possess genuine structural reasoning, or merely rely on surface-level pattern matching? The financial domain, demanding numerical precision and multi-step logic over long contexts, is an ideal testbed. Existing benchmarks fail to capture real-world industrial complexity, predominantly relying on multiple-choice questions or single-hop QA over cropped tables while ignoring intricate cross-statement dynamics and temporal de-cumulation.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce FinIndices, a large-scale benchmark evaluating data-processing fidelity over uncropped financial statements (up to 32K tokens). Utilizing an automated synthesis pipeline with adversarial traps, FinIndices encompasses Single-Index computation and Table-Index tabulation to test complex domain, temporal, and caliber reasoning.
  Our evaluation reveals two severe LLM vulnerabilities. First, a "Knowledge Bottleneck": despite memorizing formulas during pre-training, models demonstrate fragile pattern matching. Removing explicit formula hints causes performance to collapse (e.g., Gemini-3.1-Pro drops from 70.70% to 38.22% on table tasks), exposing fatal flaws in temporal de-cumulation and stock-flow caliber mismatch. Second, a "Structural Bottleneck": the intense cognitive load of generating multi-metric, multi-period tables actively drains reasoning capacity. Under structural pressure, LLMs that flawlessly execute isolated derivations regress to shallow heuristics, such as fetching incorrect adjacent columns or substituting deep accounting adjustments with lazy literal arithmetic. Finally, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) yields substantial zero-hint gains (+8.54% Single, +3.82% Table), validating that structured logic can be partially restored via data-centric alignment.

</details>


### [11] [The Checking Problem: What must be true before AI ships in a regulated firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28666)
*Prerit Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文通过在受监管金融服务场景下的6个文档密集型工作流上实验，对比4种模型家族和3种工具配置，共72种配置，发现仅56.1%配置能通过生产级标准（持续准确性、可复现性、可验证归因、置信度信号）。研究量化了人工审核负担：无置信度工具需100%审核；加引用和置信度可降至49%且保持误差容忍；自验证虽降至44%但延迟增加2.3倍且唯一破坏误差容忍。核心结论：AI工作流价值取决于人工仍需检查的比例，而非正确率，且该指标可测量但鲜被测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI项目高比例停滞，但缺乏系统性度量机制。论文旨在量化文档密集型工作流在生产环境中的真实表现，揭示演示级通过与生产级通过的差距，并度量不同工具配置对人工审核负担的影响，为AI价值评估提供实证依据。

Method: 在受监管金融服务的6个文档密集型工作流上，跨4个模型家族、3种工具配置各运行3次，共72配置产生5093个评分输出。每配置双重评估：演示标准（单案例单次正确运行）与生产标准（持续准确性、可复现性、可验证归因、置信度信号）。采用样本外估计而非事后归因计算审核负担。

Result: 57/72配置通过演示标准，仅32/72通过生产标准（通过率56.1%）。无置信度工具需100%人工审核；要求引用来源并给出置信度可将审核比例降至49%，且在20中17配置保持残差误差容忍；增加自验证步骤延迟增加2.3倍，审核比例44%，但成为唯一破坏误差容忍的配置。

Conclusion: AI工作流的实际价值不取决于正确频率，而取决于人类仍需检查的输出比例；该“审核负担”属性可量化测量，但极少被系统测量。工具设计应优先提供可验证归因和校准良好的置信度信号，而非单纯追求准确率或引入高延迟的自验证机制。

Abstract: Enterprise AI programmes stall at a rate that is widely quoted and poorly explained. This paper measures the mechanism. Six document-heavy workflows of the kind performed daily in regulated financial services were run across four model families and three tool configurations, three times each, producing 5,093 scored output elements across 72 configurations. Each configuration was assessed twice: against a demonstration bar, being a single correct run on a single case, and against a production bar requiring sustained accuracy, reproducibility across repeats, verifiable attribution, and a confidence signal that carries information. 57 of 72 configurations cleared the demonstration bar and 32 cleared the production bar, a survival rate of 56.1%. The paper then computes the review burden each configuration imposes, estimated out of sample rather than with hindsight. A tool that states no confidence requires review of 100% of its output, because it offers a reviewer no basis for triage. Requiring the tool to cite its sources and state a confidence reduces that to 49% while holding the residual error tolerance in 17 of 20 configurations. Adding a self-verification pass costs 2.3 times the latency of the plain configuration, reaches 44%, and is the only configuration that fails to hold the error tolerance. The practical implication is that the value of an AI workflow is set less by how often it is right than by how much of it a human must still check, and that the second property is measurable and rarely measured.

</details>


### [12] [TELLER: Dual-Path Iterative Preference Optimization for Table Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28680)
*Yixin Peng,Kehao Li,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TELLER 提出了一种基于迭代偏好学习的表格实体链接方法，包含直接回答路径（基于残差错误刷新偏好数据）和推理路径（长度归一化正则化偏好优化），在 TableInstruct 和 MammoTab V2 基准上均显著提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格实体链接方法依赖静态训练监督：固定的偏好数据无法适应不断演化模型的残差错误，且推理长度的变化会偏向序列级偏好学习，限制了模型性能的进一步提升。

Method: TELLER 包含两条并行路径：(1) 直接回答路径——检索并排序 Wikidata 候选实体，保留精简表格证据，应用迭代直接偏好优化（DPO），并利用更新后模型的残差错误刷新偏好数据；(2) 推理路径——使用过滤压缩的思维链基本原理进行监督微调，随后施行迭代长度归一化正则化偏好优化。

Result: 直接回答路径在 TableInstruct 上将准确率从 94.35% 提升至 94.50%，在 MammoTab V2 上从 87.59% 提升至 88.20%；推理路径在 TableInstruct 上从 92.90% 提升至 92.95%，在 MammoTab V2 上从 79.09% 大幅提升至 81.85%，同时保持了高比例的完整推理生成率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，迭代偏好学习既有利于简洁的实体预测，也有利于显式推理过程，为表格实体链接任务提供了更有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Entity linking in tables matches short and ambiguous cell mentions to their corresponding knowledge-base entities. Existing approaches typically rely on data preprocessing pipelines that retain either compact or extensive table content as contextual evidence, and then formulate entity linking as a language generation task for instruction-tuned models; recent systems further incorporate explicit reasoning to disambiguate challenging mentions. However, their training supervision is usually static: fixed preference data cannot adapt to the residual errors of an evolving model, while variations in reasoning length can bias sequence-level preference learning. To address these limitations, we present TELLER: Table Entity Linking through Learning from Errors and Reasoning. We first retrieve and rank Wikidata candidates and retain reduced table evidence in the prompt. The direct-answer path applies iterative direct preference optimization and refreshes its preference data with residual errors from the updated model. The reasoning path uses filtered and compressed chain-of-thought rationales for supervised fine-tuning, followed by our iterative length-normalized regularized preference optimization. On the TableInstruct entity-linking subset, the direct-answer path improves accuracy from 94.35\% to 94.50\%; on the MammoTab V2 evaluation set, it improves accuracy from 87.59\% to 88.20\%. The reasoning path improves accuracy from 92.90\% to 92.95\% on TableInstruct and from 79.09\% to 81.85\% on MammoTab V2, while maintaining high rates of complete reasoning generation. These results show that iterative preference learning benefits both concise entity prediction and explicit reasoning.

</details>


### [13] [Benchmarks Are Not Monolithic: Sample-Level Auditing and Orchestration for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28801)
*Philipp D. Siedler,Jordan Sassoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以数据集为中心的元评估框架，在样本层面沿五个潜在维度审计基准数据集，揭示了内部异质性，并支持基于标准的跨数据集复合子集编排，实现针对性能力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据集通常被视为单一任务，掩盖了单个样本在认知需求、语言质量、任务属性、上下文及伦理安全等方面的巨大差异，导致聚合准确率无法捕捉内部异质性，亟需一种细粒度的数据集内省方法。

Method: 构建包含五个潜在维度（认知与知识需求、语言与内容质量、任务属性、上下文、伦理安全与公平性）的元评估框架，对MMLU、ARC、WinoGrande、HellaSwag、TruthfulQA五大基准进行样本级标注，并基于标注实现跨数据集的标准驱动复合子集编排。

Result: 标注揭示了基准内部显著的异质性，聚合准分无法捕捉；基于标注的复合子集编排支持对推理深度、伦理敏感性等特定模型能力的靶向评估。

Conclusion: 该方法将基准评估重构为数据集内省，为分析和重组现有基准提供了原则性方法论，以更好地反映多样化的评估需求。

Abstract: Benchmark datasets are central to evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs), yet they are typically conceived as monolithic tasks, obscuring substantial variation in the demands of individual samples. We introduce a dataset-centric meta-evaluation framework that audits benchmark datasets at the sample level along five latent dimensions: 1. Cognitive and Knowledge Demands, 2. Language and Content Quality, 3. Task Properties, 4. Context, and 5. Ethics, Safety, and Fairness. Applying this framework, we annotate five influential benchmarks -- MMLU, ARC, WinoGrande, HellaSwag, and TruthfulQA -- revealing pronounced internal heterogeneity that is not captured by aggregate accuracy scores. We show how these annotations enable criterion-driven orchestration of composite benchmark subsets across datasets, supporting targeted evaluation of model capabilities such as Reasoning Depth or Ethical Sensitivity. This approach reframes benchmark evaluation as dataset introspection, providing a principled methodology for analyzing and re-composing existing benchmarks to better reflect diverse evaluation needs.

</details>


### [14] [Rolling With Resistance: Preference-Optimized LLM Counselors Can Trade Goal Persistence for Relational Attunement in Motivational Interviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28814)
*Weiying Chen,Junlong Shen,Zhexuan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究动机性访谈（MI）中咨询师应对来访者维持谈话的两种失败模式——妥协（放弃改变议程）和对抗（争辩/指导），提出基于MITI编码的双轴评估（目标坚持GP与关系共鸣RA），并通过偏好优化（DPO）惩罚单一失败模式，考察是否能教会“随顺抵抗”（双高）还是诱发相反失败。实验发现：惩罚对抗会稳健降低GP（代价），RA增益依赖底座模型；惩罚妥协无效，因模型极少妥协；仅提示词控制即可提升RA而无GP损失，揭示损失源于优化而非共鸣本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动机性访谈中，咨询师面对来访者的维持谈话需“随顺抵抗”，但易陷入妥协或对抗两种对立失败。现有评估缺乏双轴量化，且不知偏好优化惩罚单一失败是否能习得理想行为或反诱发另一失败。

Method: 基于专家标注的AnnoMI语料构建主题不相交的DPO偏好数据，偏好集仅在拒绝的失败类型上不同，使用on-policy负样本。训练自动评判器并经专家标签验证与人工复核，在防火墙机制下（不相交模型族分别生成、标注、评判）进行盲配对胜率测试。实验覆盖Qwen与Llama两大家族的三个对齐指令模型。

Result: 惩罚对抗在所有底座模型和所有随机种子下均显著降低目标坚持（GP），关系共鸣（RA）增益仅出现在三个底座中的两个。惩罚妥协无效，因模型on-policy极少妥协。仅提示词控制即可提升RA而无GP损失，证明GP损失源于优化过程而非共鸣目标本身。

Conclusion: 偏好优化惩罚单一失败模式存在非对称代价：惩罚对抗以牺牲目标坚持为代价换取共鸣提升，且共鸣提升不稳定；惩罚妥协因基线模型极少妥协而无效。提示词工程可在无GP损失下提升RA，提示优化本身引入了目标坚持的权衡。

Abstract: In Motivational Interviewing (MI), a client's sustain talk (arguments for the status quo) calls for the counselor to roll with resistance, a move that can fail in two opposite ways: capitulation (abandoning the change agenda to preserve rapport) or confrontation (arguing or directing, overriding the client's autonomy). We introduce a two-axis evaluation of counselor responses, anchored in the Motivational Interviewing Treatment Integrity (MITI) code, Goal Persistence (GP) and Relational Attunement (RA), yielding a four-quadrant framing in which rolling with resistance is high on both, and we ask whether penalizing one failure through preference optimization teaches rolling with resistance or provokes its opposite. From the expert-annotated AnnoMI corpus we build topic-disjoint Direct Preference Optimization data whose preference sets differ only in which failure is rejected, using on-policy negatives. An automatic judge, validated against AnnoMI's expert labels and rechecked by trained human coders, scores blind pairwise win-rates against each base under a firewall in which disjoint model families generate, label, and judge. Across three aligned instruction models spanning the Qwen and Llama families, penalizing confrontation reliably lowers goal persistence below parity, on every base and in every seed run, a robust cost, whereas the attunement gain is base-dependent, present on two of the three bases but absent on the third. Penalizing capitulation is inert, because these models rarely capitulate on-policy, so the trade is gated by each base's failure profile. A prompt-only control raises attunement without the goal-persistence cost, locating the cost in the optimization rather than in attunement itself.

</details>


### [15] [Benchmarks Are Not Validation: A System-Level View of Financial LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28840)
*Burak Payzun,İrem Demirtaş,Simona Scala,Elena Ferretti,Seçil Arslan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文主张金融领域的大语言模型系统不应仅凭基准测试分数批准上线，而需建立跨应用栈的系统级验证体系，涵盖数据、模型设计、检索生成、代理行为、治理及实现等层面，并提出混合评估、LLM-as-a-judge 控制机制、持续验证等方向，呼吁从一次性模型评分转向持续的系统验证纪律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前金融领域大语言模型应用评估过于以模型为中心，依赖基准分数、任务准确率或一次性定性审查，无法捕捉检索失败、不忠实生成、工具滥用、升级错误及运维不稳定等系统级故障模式，导致生产环境风险不可控。

Method: 基于行业验证经验，提出多层验证视角：数据层、模型设计层、检索与生成性能层、代理行为层、治理层、实现层；倡导混合评估，结合静态基准、动态系统测试、LLM-as-a-judge（需多裁判、评分规则、一致性审计等控制），并强调验证应产出决策级证据而非单一分数。

Result: 明确了金融大语言模型系统验证需从一次性模型评分转向持续的系统验证纪律，识别出静态基准难以捕捉的五大故障模式，给出 LLM-as-a-judge 的适用边界与控制要求，并形成四大研究议程：系统感知基准、代理轨迹验证、裁判对齐协议、生命周期验证标准。

Conclusion: 金融大语言模型上线审批必须建立系统级、持续性、证据驱动的验证框架，超越模型中心主义，将验证纳入全生命周期治理，确保决策就绪的证据输出，推动行业形成统一的验证标准与最佳实践。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in financial applications that combine retrieval, proprietary data, tool use, orchestration logic, monitoring, and human escalation. Yet evaluation often remains model-centric: benchmark scores, task accuracy, or one-off qualitative reviews are treated as evidence of readiness. In financial settings, this is insufficient. We take the position that financial LLM systems should not be approved for production based on benchmark performance alone. They require system-level validation evidence across the application stack: data, model design, retrieval and generation performance, agent behavior, governance, and implementation. Drawing on industry experience validating GenAI applications in financial institutions, we outline a multi-layer validation view and explain why hybrid evaluation is necessary. We discuss where LLM-as-a-judge methods are useful and why they require controls such as multiple judges, rubrics, agreement, and auditability checks. We also highlight failure modes poorly captured by static benchmarks, including retrieval failures, unfaithful generation, tool misuse, escalation errors, and operational instability. Our position is that financial LLM validation should be an ongoing system discipline rather than a one-time model scoring exercise. Validation should produce decision-ready evidence, not only scores. We conclude with a research agenda for system-aware benchmarks, agent trace validation, judge alignment protocols, and lifecycle validation standards.

</details>


### [16] [Tokenizer-Agnostic Engram Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29065)
*Jia Peng Lim,Hai Leong Chieu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的 Engram 记忆模块哈希机制，将基于 token 级 N-gram 的 XOR 哈希替换为基于字节序列的多项式哈希，实现了跨分词器的 Engram 嵌入复用，在保持性能相当的同时达成了分词器无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deepseek 的 Engram 模块依赖 token 级 N-gram 哈希进行嵌入查找，导致与特定分词器强绑定；不同分词器的模型需从头训练 Engram 嵌入，严重限制了嵌入的可复用性和迁移性。

Method: 将 N-gram 视为从所有可能字节序列中采样潜在有用字节片段的方法，而非建模不相交的 N-gram 空间；用通用多项式哈希替代 XOR 哈希，构建跨 N 值的联合嵌入空间，使字节等价的 token 序列产生相同哈希值。

Result: 实验表明，这种简单的哈希替换在性能上与原版 Engram 相当，且成功实现了分词器无关性：字节等价的 token 序列具有哈希等价性，Engram 嵌入可在不同分词器间直接复用。

Conclusion: 通过将哈希粒度从 token 级提升至字节级，并采用多项式哈希统一嵌入空间，可在不牺牲性能的前提下消除 Engram 模块对特定分词器的依赖，显著提升了记忆嵌入的通用性和复用价值。

Abstract: Deepseek's Engram, a conditional memory module, was introduced to trade-off storage versus reasoning in large language models. However, the module relies on token-level $N$-gram hashing for Engram embedding lookup, introducing a tight coupling to the tokenizer used: a model with a different tokenizer would have to train its own Engram embeddings from scratch. To improve the reusability of Engram embeddings, we propose a change to the hashing routine, enabling compatibility between Engram models using different tokenizers. Instead of modelling disjoint $N$-gram spaces, we treat $N$-gram as a method to sample potentially useful byte sequences, from all possible byte sequences across tokens. We replace the XOR-based hashing with the general polynomial hashing with a joint embedding space across $N$. This work investigates the possible trade-offs and shows that this simple substitution produces comparable performance and achieves tokenizer-agnosticism: hash equivalence for byte-equivalent token sequences.

</details>


### [17] [Semantics of Subterfuge: Benchmarking Legal Deception Detection Against General-domain State-of-the-Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29066)
*Theekshana Samaradiwakara,Nisansa de Silva,George C. Lobb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述并对比了法律领域基于NLP的自动欺骗检测方法，统一评估了七个数据集上六种微调Transformer模型和七种大语言模型在四种提示策略下的表现，发现领域敏感性强，微调模型在数据丰富的通用领域表现优异，少样本LLM在低资源法律场景具竞争力，思维链提示往往不如直接分类，强调高风险法律场景需领域适应与可解释系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗检测在司法程序、执法和网络安全中至关重要，但人工判断准确性和可扩展性有限，NLP提供了数据驱动的替代方案，尤其需要针对法律领域的系统性评估与对比。

Method: 开展统一实证评估，覆盖七个数据集（两个法律、五个通用），对比六种微调Transformer模型与七种大语言模型，在四种提示策略（包括直接分类、少样本、思维链等）下进行综合对比。

Result: 结果显示强烈的领域敏感性：微调模型在数据丰富的通用领域表现最佳；少样本LLM在低资源法律场景保持竞争力；思维链提示往往弱于直接分类。

Conclusion: 研究凸显在高风险法律语境下，需重视领域适应与可解释系统的构建，以提升自动欺骗检测的实用性与可靠性。

Abstract: Deception detection has critical implications for legal proceedings, law enforcement, and online security. Although human judgment is limited in accuracy and scalability, Natural Language Processing (NLP) offers a data-driven alternative. We present a survey and comparative analysis of NLP-based Automatic Deception Detection (ADD) focusing on the legal domain, reviewing the evolution from feature-based machine learning to Large Language Model (LLM) approaches. We conduct a unified empirical evaluation across seven datasets (two legal, five general-domain), comparing six fine-tuned transformer models and seven LLMs under four prompting strategies. The results show strong domain sensitivity, with fine-tuned models excelling in data-rich general domains and few-shot LLMs remaining competitive in low-resource legal settings. Chain-of-Thought prompting often underperforms direct classification. These findings highlight the need for domain adaptation and interpretable systems in high-stakes legal contexts.

</details>


### [18] [Faster but Different: Diagnosing and Controlling Content Drift in Accelerated Multimodal Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29079)
*Yaoxuan Dou,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了无训练加速对基于扩散的多模态大语言模型（dMLLM）服务时一致性的影响。通过在300张真实图像上对比Fast-dLLM加速输出与未加速基线，发现置信度阈值调节虽改变解码行为但不影响基线一致性；状态刷新消融实验和图像交换干预揭示了陈旧的视觉状态和生成文本状态是导致漂移的主要因素。缩短KV缓存刷新间隔可在1.3倍加速下实现近乎完全一致；类似现象也出现在dLLM-Cache和LaViDa中，但dLLM-Cache需同时收紧两个缓存才能恢复一致性，从而失去速度优势。人工审计发现一半低一致性样本存在真实内容替换；盲评实验显示事实错误率差异为0.00（95% CI [-0.17,+0.17]），未检测到差异但也未证明事实等价。自适应或平滑刷新变体在相同算力下均不优于固定间隔。贡献在于提供了一种配对诊断方法和实现层面的一致性控制，而非准确性或安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无训练加速技术使dMLLM更易部署，但可能在服务时静默改变生成内容，引发一致性问题。现有工作缺乏系统性的配对诊断和针对性的一致性控制机制，亟需量化加速带来的内容漂移并找到可控的缓解方案。

Method: 在300张真实图像上，将Fast-dLLM加速输出与同模型未加速输出进行配对对比。采用置信度阈值调节、状态刷新消融、图像交换干预等实验，分析并行度（1.05-1.25 committed tokens/step）下的解码行为变化。测量KV缓存刷新间隔对速度-一致性权衡的影响，并扩展至dLLM-Cache和LaViDa。通过人工审计50个低一致性样本对及双盲两标注员事实错误评估，量化内容替换与事实性差异。

Result: 1) 置信度阈值调节改变解码行为但不改善基线一致性；2) 陈旧视觉状态和生成文本状态是漂移主因；3) 缩短KV缓存刷新间隔产生单调速度-一致性前沿，在1.3倍加速下达近乎完全一致；4) dLLM-Cache和LaViDa呈现相似模式，但dLLM-Cache需收紧双缓存才能恢复一致性，失去加速优势；5) 独立提示词和图像复现阈值不敏感性和刷新恢复现象；6) 人工审计发现50%低一致性样本存在真实内容替换；7) 盲评事实错误率差异为0.00（95% CI [-0.17,+0.17]），未检出差异但未证等价；8) 自适应/平滑刷新变体在同等算力下均不优于固定间隔。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个配对诊断框架和实现范围内的一致性控制方法（通过调整KV缓存刷新间隔），可在保持近乎完全一致性的同时实现适度加速。但结果仅限于测试的实现和设置，不构成准确性或安全性保证。

Abstract: Training-free acceleration makes diffusion-based multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) more deployable, but it may silently change generated content. We study this serving-time consistency problem on 300 real images, comparing Fast-dLLM outputs with the same model's unaccelerated outputs. Across the mild parallelism induced in our long-form setting (1.05--1.25 committed tokens per step), confidence-threshold tuning changes decoding behavior but not baseline agreement. State-refresh ablations and an image-swap intervention instead identify stale visual and generated-text states as contributors to drift. For the tested Fast-dLLM implementation, shortening the KV-cache refresh interval yields a monotonic speed--agreement frontier and near-exact agreement at a measured 1.3x speedup. The initial diagnosis also appears with dLLM-Cache and LaViDa, although dLLM-Cache recovers agreement only after both caches are tightened, which removes its speed advantage. Independent prompts and images reproduce the threshold-insensitivity and refresh recovery. A targeted audit finds genuine content substitution in half of 50 low-agreement pairs. In a separate blinded two-annotator evaluation, the pooled accelerated-minus-baseline factual-error difference is 0.00 (95% CI [-0.17,+0.17]); this sample detects no difference but does not establish factual equivalence. Finally, none of the tested adaptive or smoothed-refresh variants beats the fixed interval at matched compute. Our contribution is a paired diagnostic and an implementation-scoped consistency control, not an accuracy or safety guarantee.

</details>


### [19] [Can Zero-Shot LLMs Predict Child Malnutrition? A Fairness and Temporal Robustness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29082)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Md Ahshanul Haque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用GPT-4o-mini零样本预测孟加拉儿童发育迟缓，平衡准确率与监督基线相当，灵敏度更高，性别间表现一致，时间稳健，但居住地和财富群体间存在公平性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低中收入国家儿童营养不良是主要公共卫生挑战，尤其南亚地区，早期识别弱势儿童对及时干预和资源分配至关重要，故评估预训练大语言模型零样本预测儿童发育迟缓的可行性、公平性和时间稳健性。

Method: 利用2007-2022年孟加拉人口与健康调查数据，将母体、儿童、医疗、家庭特征转化为语义可解释的提示表示，评估GPT-4o-mini零样本发育迟缓预测，与随机森林基线对比，并评估跨人口统计和社会经济群体的公平性及跨调查波次的时间稳健性。

Result: 零样本GPT-4o-mini平衡准确率与监督基线相当，识别发育迟缓病例的灵敏度显著更高，儿童性别群体间表现相对一致，跨BDHS波次预测行为稳定；但居住地和家庭财富类别间存在重要公平性差异。

Conclusion: 基础模型在公共卫生预测中部署前需进一步研究公平性问题，尽管零样本大语言模型在发育迟缓预测上显示出可行性和时间稳健性。

Abstract: Child malnutrition remains a major public health challenge in low- and middle-income countries, particularly in South Asia, where early identification of vulnerable children is critical for timely intervention and resource allocation. This study aims to evaluate the feasibility, fairness, and temporal robustness of using a pretrained large language model (LLM) in a zero-shot setting for child stunting prediction using population health survey data. Using Bangladesh Demographic and Health Survey (BDHS) data collected between 2007 and 2022, we transformed maternal, child, healthcare, and household characteristics into semantically interpretable prompt-based representations and evaluated GPT-4o-mini for zero-shot stunting prediction, comparing its performance against a random forest baseline and assessing fairness across demographic and socioeconomic groups as well as temporal robustness across survey waves. The results demonstrate that zero-shot inference using GPT-4o-mini achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the supervised baseline while exhibiting substantially higher sensitivity for identifying stunting cases, relatively consistent performance across child sex groups, and stable predictive behaviour across BDHS waves; however, important fairness disparities were observed across residence and household wealth categories, highlighting the need for further investigation before deployment of foundation models in public health prediction settings.

</details>


### [20] [M3-DuplexBench: A Multi-Turn, Multilingual, Multidomain Benchmark for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29125)
*Ryo Fukuda,Atsushi Ando,Hiroki Kanagawa,Takatomo Kano,Marc Delcroix,Naohiro Tawara,Yuya Chiba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 M3-DuplexBench，一个多轮、多语言、多领域的全双工口语对话系统基准，支持英语和日语，涵盖闲聊和多轮问答，并通过多种对话上下文设置评估模型，揭示了模型特定的轮流特征、跨语言/领域的性能差距以及对话历史的混合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工口语对话系统（FDSDS）基准在多轮对话的公平比较、语言覆盖和领域覆盖方面存在不足，难以全面评估系统在自然交互（如平滑轮流、回声处理、用户插话）中的表现。

Method: 构建 M3-DuplexBench 基准，包含英语和日语两种语言，覆盖闲聊和多轮问答两个领域；设计单轮、仅用户、教师强制全上下文三种对话上下文设置，系统评估近期 FDSDS 模型在不同条件下的表现。

Result: 实验显示不同模型呈现各异的轮流特征；跨语言和跨领域存在显著性能差距；对话历史对模型行为的影响呈现混合效应（有时提升有时阻碍）。

Conclusion: M3-DuplexBench 为全双工口语对话系统提供了更全面、公平的多轮、多语言、多领域评估框架，揭示了当前模型在轮流策略、语言泛化和上下文利用方面的优劣，指导未来系统改进。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems (FDSDSs) can listen while speaking, enabling natural behaviors such as smooth turn-taking, backchannel handling, and user barge-in handling. However, fair comparisons in multi-turn conversations remain a challenge. In addition, existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of languages and dialogue domains. We propose M3-DuplexBench, a multi-turn, multilingual, multidomain benchmark for FDSDSs. M3-DuplexBench supports English and Japanese and covers both casual conversation and multi-turn question answering. In addition, we evaluate models under multiple dialogue context settings, including single-turn, user-only, and teacher-forced full-context settings, to analyze how dialogue history affects model behavior. Experiments with recent FDSDSs reveal model-specific turn-taking characteristics, clear performance gaps across languages and domains, and mixed effects of dialogue context.

</details>


### [21] [Learning Latent Reasoning Traces for Scalar Reward Models End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29185)
*Sanwoo Lee,Clive Bai,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Kun Liang,Weijie Liu,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LatentRM，一种将中间推理轨迹作为离散潜变量学习的奖励建模框架，通过端到端的在线策略优化，将深度推理评估与精确评分紧密耦合，在分布内、分布外数据集及 RLHF 实验中均优于标量、生成式及混合奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量奖励模型（RMs）依赖表层线索，难以泛化到复杂或分布外任务；生成式 RMs 虽通过长推理提升鲁棒性，但其自然语言评分缺乏数值灵活性与概率可解释性。现有混合方法采用离线多任务并行优化，无法保证生成的推理轨迹主动对齐并有益于下游标量奖励预测，存在目标错位问题。

Method: 提出 LatentRM 框架：将中间推理轨迹建模为离散潜变量，显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然。采用端到端在线策略优化潜在推理空间，使深度推理评估与精确评分紧密耦合，解决推理与评分目标不一致的问题。

Result: 在分布内、分布外数据集及 RLHF 实验中，LatentRM 在偏好建模与策略对齐任务上均超越标量 RM、生成式 RM 及混合 RM，涵盖开放式对话到复杂推理等多种任务。

Conclusion: LatentRM 通过将推理轨迹作为离散潜变量并进行端到端在线优化，成功融合了生成式 RM 的深度推理能力与标量 RM 的概率评分优势，实现了更鲁棒、可泛化的奖励建模，为大模型与人类偏好的对齐提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) are central to aligning large language models with human preferences via reinforcement learning. Although traditional scalar RMs enable efficient and probabilistic reward modeling, they rely on superficial cues that fail to generalize to complex or out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. Conversely, generative RMs leverage extensive reasoning to improve robustness on challenging tasks, but their natural language-based scores lack the numerical flexibility and probabilistic interpretability that scalar RMs offer. While recent approaches combine both paradigms through off-policy multi-task learning, such parallel optimization does not guarantee that generated reasoning traces actively align with or benefit downstream scalar reward prediction. To address this mismatch, we propose LatentRM, a reward modeling framework that learns intermediate reasoning traces as discrete latent variables to explicitly maximize the likelihood of downstream scalar rewards. Through on-policy optimization of the latent reasoning space end-to-end, LatentRM tightly couples deep reasoning-based evaluation with precise scoring. Extensive validations on in-distribution and OOD datasets and RLHF show that LatentRM outperforms scalar, generative, and hybrid RMs on preference modeling and policy alignment across tasks ranging from open-ended conversation to complex reasoning.

</details>


### [22] [Detecting Experiential Intertextuality Across Migration Routes: Beyond Surface Similarity in French Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29188)
*Sakayo Toadoum Sari,Nelly Robin,Michelle Auzanneau,Lakhdar Sais,Veronique Petit,Marie Veniard,Said Jabbour,Fabien Delorme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“经验互文性检测”任务，旨在无需标注训练数据的情况下，自动识别跨越不同移民路线（如跨撒哈拉和巴尔干走廊）的法语移民叙事中共享的经验回响。研究使用108篇叙事生成句对，采用词汇基线、句嵌入、词性结构特征、移民主题词典、语境感知叙事特征及零样本大语言模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B）等31种无标注方法打分，并针对816个人工标注判断进行验证。结果显示：表层、结构及嵌入方法与专家判断相关性均较弱（r≤0.30）；Qwen2.5-7B零样本表现最佳（r=0.38）；少样本提示会降低Qwen性能但显著提升Mistral；叙事位置（特别是出发阶段）显著预测互文性；融合全部31特征的监督混合模型达到r=0.45，比最佳单一方法提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移民在不同地理路线上常经历相似的创伤（警暴、偷渡剥削、危险穿越、家庭分离），但缺乏大规模标注数据来训练模型自动发现这些跨语料的共享经验回响。现有互文性检测多依赖表层相似度或需监督数据，难以捕捉深层经验层面的共鸣。因此需要一种无标注、可解释的方法来量化叙事间的经验互文性。

Method: 1. 收集108篇法语移民叙事（跨撒哈拉与巴尔干走廊），自动生成句对。2. 设计31种无标注打分方法：词汇重叠基线、句嵌入（SBERT等）、词性结构特征、移民专用主题词典、语境感知叙事特征（如叙事阶段位置）、零样本大语言模型打分（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B，三种提示策略）。3. 邀请专家对816个句对进行互文性标注（Krippendorff's α=0.27），作为金标准。4. 计算各方法分数与专家判断的相关性，并训练监督混合模型融合所有特征。

Result: • 表层、结构、嵌入方法与专家判断相关性均弱（r≤0.30）。• Qwen2.5-7B零样本达到最佳单一方法相关性（r=0.38）。• 少样本提示损害Qwen但大幅提升Mistral性能。• 叙事位置显著预测互文性，出发阶段句对经验回响最强。• 融合31特征的监督混合模型达r=0.45，比最佳单一方法提升21%。

Conclusion: 经验互文性检测不能仅靠表层或结构相似度捕捉，需结合领域知识（主题词典）、叙事语境及大语言模型的语义理解。零样本大模型虽优于传统无监督方法，但仍有提升空间；少样本提示效果因模型而异。融合多源特征的监督混合模型显著提升性能，证明了多视角特征互补的价值。未来可探索更强的大模型、更细粒度的叙事建模及跨语言迁移。

Abstract: Migrants traversing geographically distinct routes such as the Trans-Saharan and Balkan corridors often recount strikingly parallel lived experiences: police violence, smuggler exploitation, dangerous crossings, and family separation. We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [23] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hy-MultiTurn，一个面向长轮次中文对话理解的基准测试，基于真实聊天机器人失败案例提炼出六大失效机制，构建 209 个受控任务（12-76 轮），评测 22 个前沿模型显示即使最强的 GPT-5.5 也仅在 41.1% 的响应中完全满足要求，且无模型在六大模式中均表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮对话基准多局限于短对话，难以全面评估模型在长轮次交互中记忆约束、追踪修正、指代消歧、抑制动作等深层理解能力，尤其缺乏中文基准，且难以揭示模型失败的具体机制。

Method: 通过分析真实聊天机器人失败案例，归纳出六类反复出现的失效机制，据此定义六种受控评测模式：约束记忆、精准执行、约束综合、对象定位、动作抑制、指代消歧。构建 209 个受控任务，对话轮数跨度 12-76 轮，并引入长对话、无关话题干扰、口语化表达等额外难度维度。

Result: 在 22 个前沿模型配置上的评测表明，Hy-MultiTurn 普遍具有挑战性：最强配置 GPT-5.5 仅在 41.1% 的响应中完全满足所有要求，且没有任何模型在六大评测模式中均达到最优。

Conclusion: Hy-MultiTurn 通过从真实失效中提炼机制并转化为受控评测模式，填补了长轮次中文多轮对话深度理解评测的空白，揭示了当前最先进模型在复杂多轮交互中仍存在显著不足，为后续模型改进提供了细粒度的诊断依据。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

</details>


### [24] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在超出能力范围任务上产生的“徒劳推理”现象，并提出CaRL方法通过能力对齐强化学习减少无效推理，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在超出能力边界的任务上会生成计算成本高但语义空洞的推理，这种“徒劳推理”听起来合理实则错误，会误导用户。现有模型存在普遍的能力越界行为，且能力与行为之间存在系统性误校准，主要失效模式为看似有效实含微妙错误的“似是而非推理”，且随任务难度增加而加剧。

Method: 提出CaRL（能力对齐强化学习），通过两大机制实现模型行为与能力边界对齐：1) 奖励塑形，激励模型在超出能力任务上选择拒绝而非徒劳推理；2) 事后拒绝增强，将推理失败转化为拒绝监督信号，强化模型识别能力边界并主动拒绝的能力。

Result: 实验表明，CaRL显著减少了徒劳推理的发生，同时在不同难度任务上保持了模型性能，有效实现了能力对齐行为而不牺牲实用性。

Conclusion: 通过能力对齐强化学习，可以有效缓解大语言模型在超出能力范围任务上的徒劳推理问题，使模型学会在能力边界处主动拒绝，从而提高可靠性和安全性，同时保持在能力范围内的任务性能。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>


### [25] [Small Is Enough: Per-User Style Rewriting of AI-Edited Text via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29238)
*Antorweep Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InMyStyle 是一个以隐私为先的单用户系统，通过在本地微调 LoRA 适配器，将 0.5B-7B 参数的小语言模型适配为无需推理时提示词即可将 AI 编辑文本重写为个人写作风格。实验显示综合得分在所有模型规模上均稳定在 0.69，说明小模型已足够完成该任务；LLM 评委评分表明输出的“AI 感”比输入降低 20% 以上，且随模型增大进一步下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本风格迁移方法往往依赖大模型、需要推理时提示词或无法保证隐私。作者旨在构建一个隐私优先、单用户、无需推理时指令即可将 AI 生成文本改写为个人写作风格的系统。

Method: 利用用户本地文档，配合多个本地辅助 LLM 构建配对训练样本，在 0.5B 到 7B 参数的基座模型上微调 LoRA 适配器；引入长度感知的生成预算与自动分块机制以支持变长输入。

Result: 在科学论文语料的 219 个评估对上，自动综合得分在贪婪与采样解码下均稳定在 0.69（0-1），跨模型规模出现平台期；400 次五位 LLM 评委打分显示，InMyStyle 输出的平均感知 AI 度比辅助 AI 生成的输入低 20% 以上，且随模型规模增大进一步降低。

Conclusion: 小模型（0.5B-7B）已足以完成个性化重写任务，模型规模决定的是速度/显存与质量的权衡，而非稳定的质量排序；InMyStyle 实现了隐私优先、无需提示词的有效风格适配，并显著降低了输出文本的“AI 感”。

Abstract: InMyStyle is a privacy first, single user system that adapts small language models to rewrite AI-edited text towards an individual user's writing style without an instruction prompt at inference. Given a user's documents, it uses multiple local helper LLMs to construct paired training examples and fine tunes LoRA adapters on base models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters. Length aware generation budgets and automatic chunking support inputs of different lengths. On 219 evaluation pairs from a scientific-paper corpus, the automatic composite score plateaus at 0.69 [scale 0-1] across all model sizes under both greedy and sampled decoding. This observed plateau suggests that small models are sufficient for the measured rewriting task, with model size determining trade-offs rather than a stable quality ranking. As a secondary evaluation, 400 ratings from five LLM judges give InMyStyle outputs a mean perceived AI-ness score over 20% lower than their helper-AI generated inputs, while mean perceived AI-ness scores decrease with model size within InMyStyle.

</details>


### [26] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Data Turnstile是一个开源框架，通过将用户定义的API规范转化为高质量合成训练数据，解决小语言模型（SLM）在工具使用任务中面临的数据稀缺和噪声问题。该框架将多轮工具使用交互分解为受约束的逐步生成，并结合验证和错误反馈循环，实现对API多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性的细粒度控制。实验表明，使用Turnstile数据微调的Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-1.7B模型在BFCL单轮基准和τ²-bench多轮代理基准上显著超越同规模基座模型，甚至接近或超过大得多的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）因低延迟、低成本和设备端隐私优势适合代理部署，但在工具使用任务上表现不佳，主要瓶颈在于高质量训练数据稀缺且嘈杂。与大模型不同，SLM无法通过巨大参数量补偿低质量监督，因此数据质量成为关键限制因素。

Method: 提出Data Turnstile框架：输入用户定义的API规范，将多轮工具使用交互分解为受约束的逐步生成过程，引入验证机制和错误反馈循环，实现对API多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性的细粒度控制，生成高质量合成函数调用训练数据。

Result: 在BFCL单轮基准上，Turnstile微调的Qwen3-0.6B达75.9%准确率（基座模型思考模式67.4%），接近Qwen3-1.7B（78.4%）和Qwen3-4B（79.9%）。在τ²-bench多轮基准Telecom领域，Turnstile微调的Qwen3-1.7B达31.1% pass¹（基座6.6%，提升4.7倍），超越Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct（27.4%）；Qwen3-0.6B达24.6%（基座3.5%，提升7倍），接近32B模型。

Conclusion: Data Turnstile通过结构化合成数据生成有效解决了SLM工具使用能力受限于数据质量的问题，使小模型在单轮和多轮函数调用任务上均能大幅缩小与大模型的性能差距，为资源受限场景下的代理部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. Turnstile decomposes multi-turn tool-use interactions into constrained, stepwise generation with validation and error-feedback loops, providing fine-grained control over API diversity, conversation complexity, and output correctness. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>


### [27] [CalibratedRubric: Task-Adaptive Rubric Banks for Open-Ended LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29252)
*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CalibratedRubric 是一个任务自适应框架，结合类型特定评分、贝叶斯量规可测性过滤和基于项目反应理论（IRT）的题库组装，用于高效、不确定性感知的开放式大语言模型输出评估。实验表明，可测性过滤将人工金标准一致性从 κ=0.604 提升至 0.743，IRT 贪婪选择仅需 49 条量规即可达到 131 条的目标相关性，且任务标签扰动验证了任务自适应评分的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式大语言模型输出的可靠评估需要细粒度量规，但专家策划成本高且难以规模化。现有自动化流程依赖严格的评判员一致性和二元方差过滤，无法区分可测量量规与信息量大的量规。

Method: 提出 CalibratedRubric 框架，包含三大组件：1) 类型特定评分，2) 基于 Beta-Bernoulli 协议后验的贝叶斯量规可测性过滤，3) 基于子模块信息覆盖目标的 IRT 题库组装。通过估计每条量规的可测性，构建覆盖观测能力范围的紧凑量规库。

Result: 在金融、医疗、通用和法律基准上，可测性过滤将 JudgmentBench 上的人工金标准一致性从 κ=0.604 提升至 0.743。IRT 贪婪选择在所有六个评估响应块上均优于随机选择，且在 FinResearchBench 决策支持任务中仅需 49 条量规即可达到目标相关性（随机选择需 131 条）。任务标签扰动进一步降低了系统分离度，证实任务自适应评分的实际相关性。

Conclusion: CalibratedRubric 是一种高效、不确定性感知的开放式大语言模型评估方法，其校准增益依赖于足够的评判员冗余。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended LLM outputs requires fine-grained rubrics, yet expert curation is costly and difficult to scale. Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. We introduce CalibratedRubric, a task-adaptive framework that combines type-specific scoring, Bayesian rubric-measurability filtering, and item response theory (IRT)-based bank assembly. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>


### [28] [Translation with Thought: Difficulty-Adaptive Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29287)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出TwT（Translation with Thought），一种资源理性的多领域机器翻译框架，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+强化学习）学习在直觉和深思熟虑推理间动态调节推理力度，在15个基准、3种已见和59种未见语言上超越更大规模SOTA推理模型，同时推理token消耗降低32-60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域机器翻译面临不同领域语言复杂度差异大的挑战。受人类翻译员根据难度调节推理投入的启发，旨在开发一种能像人类一样在直觉式快速翻译和深思熟虑式详细推理间自适应切换的模型，兼顾翻译质量与推理效率。

Method: 两阶段训练：1) 监督微调：使用从DeepSeek-R1蒸出并经GPT-4o重写为符合人类推理经济特性的难度感知长思维链轨迹进行SFT；2) 强化学习：采用混合奖函数联合优化翻译质量与推理效率。在三个不同骨干模型上进行消融实验。

Result: TwT-7B和TwT-14B在15个基准（含域内/域外、3种已见及59种未见语言）上翻译质量超越显著更大的SOTA推理模型，推理token用量减少32%-60%。消融实验验证两阶段训练各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 将翻译行为与认知原理（资源理性、自适应推理深度）对齐，能实现多领域机器翻译的鲁棒泛化、高翻译质量和高推理效率，为大模型翻译提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>


### [29] [Cross-Lingual Transfer for Machine Translation in Turkic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29355)
*Omer Burak Cinar,Mehmet Mert Dalkilic,Cagri Toraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了五种突厥语言（土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语、吉尔吉斯语）之间的跨语言迁移，使用成对迁移矩阵和mT5模型，发现迁移效果在近亲语言对间最强（土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语、哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语），迁移方向很重要，拉丁化在文字不匹配设置下能提升BLEU和chrF但效果不一致，迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下基本稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言迁移是低资源机器翻译的核心，但在紧密相关语言家族内部的迁移行为尚未得到充分刻画。论文旨在系统分析突厥语族内部的迁移模式。

Method: 采用成对迁移矩阵方法：每个模型用一种迁移源微调，在不同迁移目标上评估，而翻译目标保持不变。使用mT5模型在五种突厥语言上进行实验。

Result: 1) 迁移在近亲突厥语对间最强（土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语、哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语）；2) 迁移方向有显著影响；3) 相同迁移源-目标对在翻译目标改变时表现不同；4) 拉丁化在文字不匹配设置下提升BLEU和chrF，但效果在指标间不一致；5) 迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下基本稳定。

Conclusion: 突厥语族内的跨语言迁移遵循语言亲缘关系，方向性很重要，文字统一（拉丁化）有帮助但不稳定，迁移源选择具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [30] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Zero-Mem提出了一种零Token记忆操作机制，无需在最终问答之外调用LLM，通过实体-上下文图和时间层级双视角组织交互轨迹，实现高效且准确的长程记忆问答，显著降低时间和Token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统常依赖额外LLM调用生成中间记录和检索，导致持续的Token和时间开销，且可能丢失原始证据细节。研究旨在探讨结构化记忆访问是否必须依赖生成过程，提出完全无需LLM生成的记忆操作方案。

Method: Zero-Mem保留原始交互轨迹作为记录源，构建实体-上下文图（跨交互连接）和时间层级（保持会话局部性和状态）两种互补视角。查询时加权双视角、双向检索并沿结构回溯支持关系或上下文。确定性校准先丢弃冲突证据，再将读取器答案锚定在检索轨迹上。仅最终问答读取器调用LLM。

Result: 在长记忆和长上下文问答基准上，Zero-Mem性能具竞争力，完全消除记忆操作中的LLM调用和Token消耗；在相同最终读取器和上下文预算下，记忆操作时间成本较最快基线降低57.6%。消融实验验证双视角及其查询依赖协调的贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化智能体记忆无需生成过去的中间表征，零Token记忆操作可同时实现高效检索与准确问答，为长程交互记忆提供新范式。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [31] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒设置下的LLM反演方法，通过从目标LLM合成的数据中从头训练一个显式的逆语言模型，利用前向token预测实现忠实的提示词重构，无需外部数据或模型权重访问，且支持多样化重构且泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM反演方法依赖微调预训练序列到序列模型，需要大量外部数据及模型权重或logits访问，将提示词恢复视为语义重构任务，难以在黑盒设置下实现。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出一种无需辅助资源的功能性反演方法。

Method: 从目标LLM合成数据，从头训练显式逆语言模型，采用前向token预测（类比前向next-token prediction）建立前向与逆过程的生成联系，实现忠实提示词重构；支持采样生成多样化提示词重构。

Result: 方法在多个数据集上泛化，展现出迁移性，能重构不同LLM生成的响应对应的提示词；在基于token的评估指标上优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 该黑盒LLM反演方法通过自监督训练逆语言模型，实现了无需外部资源的忠实提示词重构，具有泛化性和迁移性，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [32] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个翻译模型家族 EuroLLM 和 Hy-MT2（1.7B 到 22B 参数）在单张 A100/H100 GPU 上的量化权衡。结果显示：结合文档分块策略与 W4A8/W8A8 量化可改善延迟-吞吐率帕累托曲线；标准句级评测无法预测量化与长上下文文档翻译的交互；Hy-MT2 在量化下保持鲁棒，而 EuroLLM 对量化极度敏感且质量迅速崩塌。推理效率与翻译质量的权衡不仅取决于量化格式，还取决于文本分块策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实服务器环境部署大语言模型需要低延迟的高质量响应。量化虽能减小内存占用并提升推理效率，但其在编排级负载下对延迟和吞吐率的影响鲜有系统评估。此外，标准机器翻译基准依赖孤立句子，无法捕捉长上下文动态，导致无法准确评估量化在文档级翻译中的表现。

Method: 选取 EuroLLM 和 Hy-MT2 两个模型家族共五个模型（1.7B–22B），在单张 A100 或 H100 GPU 上进行实验。采用文档分块策略结合 W4A8 与 W8A8 量化格式，测量延迟-吞吐率帕累托曲线。引入 WMT24++ 文档级评测集，对比不同分块策略在量化条件下对翻译质量的影响，并与传统句级评测对比。

Result: 1) 文档分块 + W4A8/W8A8 量化在多种负载下均改善了延迟-吞吐率帕累托曲线。2) 标准句级评测无法预测量化与长上下文交互导致的质量变化。3) Hy-MT2 在所有量化格式下表现鲁棒；EuroLLM 则极度敏感，翻译质量在所有量化格式下均快速崩塌。4) 推理效率与翻译质量的权衡同时取决于量化格式与文本分块策略。

Conclusion: 量化带来的效率收益与质量损失具有强模型依赖性和上下文依赖性。文档级评测对于评估真实部署场景至关重要。Hy-MT2 更适合量化后的生产部署，而 EuroLLM 在量化下不可靠。最佳部署配置需联合考虑量化精度与分块策略。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [33] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型智能体在个性化陪伴中面临知识利用问题：即使用户偏好已在上下文中，模型也可能无法据此行动。本文提出解耦评估范式，通过配对的Know和Act测试分离记忆与利用能力，大规模实验显示两者存在显著差距，记忆架构虽能缩小差距但在健康治疗相关偏好上利用仍薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型智能体演变为个性化伴侣，记忆成为核心能力，但模型可能无法利用上下文中已有的用户偏好，不清楚是记忆失败还是利用失败，需要隔离这一故障点。

Method: 引入解耦评估范式，对同一用户偏好施配对的Know（回忆）和Act（行为）测试。跨16个系统和5种记忆架构，评估1000个偏好在三种表达强度下的嵌入情况。

Result: 实验显示Know和Act结果存在巨大差距：智能体常通过偏好回忆测试，但在配对行为场景中未能体现该偏好。记忆架构可减小差距，但健康治疗相关偏好的利用仍特别薄弱，而这些失败后果最严重。

Conclusion: 大语言模型智能体存在知识利用鸿沟，记忆架构虽有助于缩小差距，但在高风险领域（如健康治疗）的偏好利用仍不足，需进一步改进记忆利用机制。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [34] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现，在辛普森悖论环境中，增加干预数据预训练比例并不能改善因果方向预测；模型因果推理能力取决于推理时上下文中的证据类型，观察性上下文会抑制因果插值能力，而干预性上下文能释放该能力。该抑制机制稳定且可定位至中间层的观察性行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究干预数据作为因果推理教学黄金标准的假设，在完全可控的合成环境中测试观察性相关性与因果效应系统性相反（辛普森悖论）时，模型是否能学会正确的因果方向。

Method: 构建合成辛普森悖论世界，控制预训练中干预样本比例，评估模型在不同推理上下文（纯观察性、混合、纯干预）下的 do()-响应符号与大小；进行激活修补定位抑制开关；在 CLadder 基准上进行外部审计；量化探针评估噪声并提出证据平均协议。

Result: 增加干预预训练数据不改善因果方向，符号仍复制自观察性上下文；纯观察性上下文导致 29/50 世界符号反转，混合上下文 19/50，纯干预探针 41/50 正确；移除观察性证据即释放因果插值能力（ratio_true=+0.56）；抑制跨种子稳定（11/11 持续），在 0.93B 参数下鲁棒（31.8% vs 6% 反转）；外部审计发现习得正效应先验，两层结构，符号随机化重训仅在分布内移除；激活修补定位开关至中间层观察性行；证据平均协议将符号错误从 26% 降至 9%。

Conclusion: 因果推理能力编码在模型权重中，但由推理时上下文中的证据类型控制开启/关闭；观察性上下文会抑制因果插值，干预性上下文则激活之；该机制稳定、可定位，且可通过证据平均协议显著降低评估噪声。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [35] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB)，评估AI文本检测器在人类文本被LLM重写时的表现，发现传统人类-vs-LLM基准上的性能无法迁移到该场景：检测器对H2L文本召回率大幅下降，但对LLM2L文本保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准仅比较人类文本与直接LLM生成文本，未考察人类文经LLM重写后的检测效果，需填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建ARB基准，包含1800个人类源文本（XSum、WritingPrompts、OpenWebText各600），使用四个开源LLM（Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B）生成四种变体：HUMAN、Free-LLM、H2L、LLM2L；在1% FPR操作点评估五种检测器。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b对直接LLM文本召回率分别为91.2%和93.5%，但对H2L文本仅30.8%和15.1%，下降60-78个百分点；对LLM2L文本保持78.3%和83.0%，下降仅10-13点。RADAR呈现类似模式。BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense全场景召回率均低于3%。

Conclusion: 传统人类-vs-LLM基准上的检测器性能无法预测其对人类重写文本的表现；检测器对LLM重写的人类文本脆弱，但对LLM重写的LLM文本鲁棒，需开发针对重写场景的检测方法。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L).
